1. **Identify the black nodes**: Nodes A and N are the black nodes.
2. **Trace possible paths**:
   - From node A to node B
   - From node B to node E, S, or F
   - From node E to node T or N
   - Therefore, one possible path is A -> B -> E -> N
   - Another path could be A -> B -> S -> N

3. **Check the lengths of these paths**:
   - Path A -> B -> E -> N has a length of 3.
   - Path A -> B -> S -> N has a length of 3.

4. **Explore other possible paths**:
   - From node A to node P
   - From node P to node I
   - From node I to node J
   - From node J to node R
   - Since there is no direct or shorter path from any of these nodes to node N, these paths are not considered.

5. **Identify the shortest path**:
   - The shortest path from A to N is through B and E, or through B and S.
   - Since both paths have the same length, we can choose either.

Therefore, the shortest path between the two black nodes is:

Length: 3, Path: [A, B, E, N]

- Removing (B, F) and (F, J) directly eliminates all paths going through F and J.
- Removing (E, F) and (B, F) might not be necessary if we focus on F -> J directly.

   - A -> E -> B -> G -> J -> H
   - A -> E -> B -> G -> J -> I -> H
   - A -> E -> B -> T -> F -> G -> J -> H

   - A -> S -> B -> G -> J -> I -> H
   - A -> S -> B -> T -> F -> G -> J -> H
   - A -> S -> B -> T -> F -> G -> J -> I -> H

   - Removing E will disconnect paths from A to B via E.
   - Removing B will disconnect all paths originating from A that go through S or E to B.
   - Removing G will disconnect paths from B to J and I.
   - Removing J will directly disconnect both paths going through G.

We observe that:
   - Removing E and B will disconnect paths from A through S and E.
   - Removing G will disconnect paths from B through T and F.
   - J will be cut off by removing either G or J directly.

However, the optimal solution is to find the smallest set of nodes to disconnect all paths. By removing E and G, we effectively cut off all paths between A and H.

Thus, the minimum number of nodes to remove is:

Nodes to remove: 2, Cut Set: [E, G]
